(d) None of the above                                     Answer: (b)

     • Consumer welfare = overall benefit or                       industrialisation and self-reliance.
         satisfaction individuals derive from                  • Industrial production grew steadily across
         consuming goods and services.                             sectors like steel, cement, fertilisers, and
     • Savings only show the part of income not                    consumer goods.
         spent; it doesn't reflect utility gained.             • GDP grew from ₹2.7 lakh crore to ₹57 lakh
     • Disposable income shows purchasing capacity                 crore, reflecting massive output expansion.
         but not how much benefit is obtained from             • Exports, infrastructure and agricultural
         spending.                                                 support contributed to increased industrial
     • Expenditure indicates spending levels, not                  output.
         necessarily the welfare outcomes.                     • While employment and diversification
     • None of these directly measures consumer                    improved too, the most striking feature has
         well-being or utility, which is what welfare              been production growth.
         truly reflects.                                       • Hence, option b is correct
     • Hence, option d is correct

power energy?
(a) Bhakra Nangal
(b) Koyna
(c) Hirakud
(d) Salal

     • Koyna Hydroelectric Project (Maharashtra) is
          India’s largest completed hydroelectric power
          plant.
     • Total installed capacity ≈ 1,960 MW.

(a) Increase in investment
(b) Increase of savings

                                                             • The President and Prime Minister have no
                                                                 role in currency issuance.
                                                             • The Governor’s signature represents the
                                                                 guarantee of the Central Bank to honour the
                                                                 note’s value.
                                                             • Hence, option b is correct

Q10. The rupee exchange rate is currently linked
with
(a) Pound Sterling
(b) Dollar
(c) Basket of currencies
(d) —
Ans. (d) (i.e., Basket of currencies)
     • By early 1980s, India shifted from a sterling
         peg to a basket-peg system to stabilise the
         rupee.
     • The exchange rate was linked to a basket of
         currencies of major trading partners.
     • This diversified currency risk compared to
         pegging with a single currency.
     • The system allowed more realistic exchange
         rate movements in line with trade patterns.
     • It was a managed floating regime, precursor

                                                                  contributing the most to the Gross Domestic
                                                                  Product (GDP).
                                                              • The Industry sector, which includes
                                                                  manufacturing, construction, and mining, is
                                                                  the second-largest contributor.
                                                              • The Agriculture sector now has the smallest
                                                                  share in the GDP, although it still employs a
                                                                  large portion of the population.

       to purchase equities listed on Indian             with which one of the following?
       exchanges.                                        (a) Increase in the Cash Reserve Ratio in the banks
   •   Hence option correct.                             (b) Increase in the Statutory Liquidity Ratio in the
                                                         banks
                                                         (c) Increase in the banking habit of the people
                                                         (d) Increase in the population of the country
                                                         The correct option is (c).
                                                              • The money multiplier is the amount of money
                                                                  created by banks for a given fixed amount of
                                                                  base money and reserve ratio. For example, if
                                                                  commercial banks gain deposits of ₹1 lakh
                                                                  and this leads to a final money supply of ₹10
                                                                  lakh, the money multiplier is 10.
                                                              • An increase in the banking habit of the
                                                                  population will increase deposits and lending,
                                                                  thereby increasing the money multiplier.
                                                              • Hence option correct.

     1. United Arab Emirates                                      gas.
     2. France                                                • However, production of crude oil and natural
     3. Germany                                                   gas is explicitly excluded.
     4. Singapore                                             • Hence, activities II and III are regulated.
     5. Bangladesh                                         Hence, option (b) is correct.
How many among the above accept international
merchant payments under UPI?
(a) Only two
(b) Only three
(c) Only four
(d) All five
The correct option is (c).
     • Accepted: UAE , France , Singapore , (plus
          Nepal among others).
     • Not accepted: Germany , Bangladesh .
     • Hence, four of the listed countries have UPI
          international merchant acceptance.
Hence, option (c) is correct.




Q15. Consider the following activities:
     1. Production of crude oil
     2. Refining, storage and distribution of
          petroleum
     3. Marketing and sale of petroleum products
     4. Production of natural gas
How many of the above activities are regulated by the
Petroleum and Natural Gas Regulatory Board in our
country?
(a) Only one
(b) Only two
(c) Only three
(d) All four
The correct option is (b).
     • The PNGRB Act, 2006 mandates regulation of
          refining, processing, storage, transportation,
          distribution, marketing, and sale of
